; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 65536, R0       ; load float constant 1.0 (Q16.16)
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FADD             ; call F_FADD
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 196608, R0      ; load float constant 3.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FMUL             ; call F_FMUL
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FDIV             ; call F_FDIV
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FADD                          ; Helper function: float add(float a, float b)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (left operand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (right operand, Q16.16)
        ADD R0, R1, R0          ; add Q16.16 values
        MOVE R0, R6             ; result (Q16.16)
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

F_FMUL                          ; Helper function: float mul(float a, float b)
; F_FMUL: Q16.16 fixed-point multiplication using 32-bit Russian peasant algorithm
; Q16.16 representable range: [-32768.0, +32767.9999]
; Safe multiplication domain: [-181.0, +181.0] per operand (no overflow)
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a * b (Q16.16 format)
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (multiplicand, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (multiplier, Q16.16)
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if a == 0, return 0
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JR_SGE L4               ; a >= 0 → nothing
L5                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L4                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
; 64-bit Russian peasant multiplication: product = |a| * |b|
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; M_HI = 0 (high 32 bits of multiplicand)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; ACC_LO = 0
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; ACC_HI = 0
L8                              ; 64-bit multiplication loop
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JR_EQ L9                ; if B == 0, exit loop
        AND R1, %D 1, R6        ; R6 = B & 1
        CMP R6, %D 0
        JR_EQ L10               ; if (B & 1) == 0, skip add
        ADD R2, R0, R2          ; ACC_LO += M_LO
        ADC R4, R3, R4          ; ACC_HI += M_HI + carry
L10                             ; skip add
        MOVE R0, R6             ; save old M_LO
        SHL R0, %D 1, R0        ; M_LO <<= 1
        SHR R6, %D 31, R6       ; R6 = (old M_LO >> 31) & 1 (carry bit)
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; M_HI <<= 1
        OR R3, R6, R3           ; M_HI |= carry_from_LO
        SHR R1, %D 1, R1        ; B >>= 1
        JR L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; multiplication done
; Q16.16 scaling: extract middle 32 bits from 64-bit product
        MOVE R2, R0             ; R0 = ACC_LO
        MOVE R4, R1             ; R1 = ACC_HI
        SHR R0, %D 16, R0       ; R0 = ACC_LO >> 16 (low 16 bits of result)
        SHL R1, %D 16, R1       ; R1 = ACC_HI << 16 (high 16 bits)
        OR R0, R1, R0           ; result_abs = (ACC_HI << 16) | (ACC_LO >> 16)
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JR_EQ L11               ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        JR_SLT L12              ; if sign < 0, negate
        JP L13                  ; sign > 0, no change
L11                             ; result is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; R0 = 0
        JP L13                  ; jump to done
L12                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R3
        SUB R3, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L13                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
        JP L3                   ; skip zero handler
L2                              ; zero operand
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for zero operand
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L3                              ; end of function

F_FDIV                          ; Helper function: float div(float a, float b)
; F_FDIV: Q16.16 fixed-point division using 32-bit arithmetic
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a / b (Q16.16 format)
; 
; CRITICAL: R5 is reserved as frame pointer and must NEVER be used
; as a general-purpose register. All temporaries use R0-R4, R6-R7.
; All conditional jumps use JP_* mnemonics (not JR_*) for consistency
; with compiler-generated code.
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (dividend, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (divisor, Q16.16)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_EQ L14               ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JP_EQ L17               ; a == 0 → sign = 0
        JP_SLT L16              ; a < 0 → negate
        JP L15                  ; a > 0 → nothing
L17                             ; a is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (result will be 0)
        JP L15
L16                             ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L15                             ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_SGE L18              ; b >= 0 → nothing
L19                             ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L18                             ; b sign handled
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L14               ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
; 32-bit unsigned division: integer_part = a_abs / b_abs
        MOVE R0, R4             ; save dividend a_abs in R4
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; integer_part = 0 (quotient)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; remainder = 0
        MOVE %D 31, R3          ; i = 31 (loop counter)
L20                             ; 32-bit integer division loop
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JP_SLT L21              ; if i < 0, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        MOVE R4, R6             ; temp = dividend (use R6 temporarily)
        SHR R6, R3, R6          ; temp = dividend >> i
        AND R6, %D 1, R6        ; temp = (dividend >> i) & 1
        OR R2, R6, R2           ; remainder |= (dividend >> i) & 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L22              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        MOVE %D 1, R6
        SHL R6, R3, R6          ; R6 = 1 << i
        OR R0, R6, R0           ; integer_part |= (1 << i)
L22                             ; skip subtraction
        SUB R3, %D 1, R3        ; i--
        JP L20                  ; continue loop
L21                             ; integer division done
; 16-step fractional refinement loop
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; frac = 0 (fractional accumulator)
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; i = 0 (loop counter, use R6 temporarily)
L23                             ; fractional refinement loop
        CMP R6, %D 16
        JP_SGE L24              ; if i >= 16, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; frac <<= 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L25              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        OR R3, %D 1, R3         ; frac |= 1
L25                             ; skip subtraction
        ADD R6, %D 1, R6        ; i++
        JP L23                  ; continue loop
L24                             ; fractional loop done
; Combine integer_part and frac into Q16.16 result
        SHL R0, %D 16, R0       ; integer_part <<= 16
        OR R0, R3, R0           ; result_abs = (integer_part << 16) | frac
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L26               ; sign == 0 → result 0
        JP_SLT L27              ; sign < 0 → negate
        JP L28                  ; sign > 0 → no change
L26                             ; sign zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        JP L28
L27                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L28                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L14                             ; division by zero or dividend is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for division by zero or zero dividend
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 393216, R0      ; load float constant 6.0 (Q16.16)
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FDIV             ; call F_FDIV
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FDIV                          ; Helper function: float div(float a, float b)
; F_FDIV: Q16.16 fixed-point division using 32-bit arithmetic
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a / b (Q16.16 format)
; 
; CRITICAL: R5 is reserved as frame pointer and must NEVER be used
; as a general-purpose register. All temporaries use R0-R4, R6-R7.
; All conditional jumps use JP_* mnemonics (not JR_*) for consistency
; with compiler-generated code.
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (dividend, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (divisor, Q16.16)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JP_EQ L5                ; a == 0 → sign = 0
        JP_SLT L4               ; a < 0 → negate
        JP L3                   ; a > 0 → nothing
L5                              ; a is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (result will be 0)
        JP L3
L4                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L3                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
; 32-bit unsigned division: integer_part = a_abs / b_abs
        MOVE R0, R4             ; save dividend a_abs in R4
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; integer_part = 0 (quotient)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; remainder = 0
        MOVE %D 31, R3          ; i = 31 (loop counter)
L8                              ; 32-bit integer division loop
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JP_SLT L9               ; if i < 0, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        MOVE R4, R6             ; temp = dividend (use R6 temporarily)
        SHR R6, R3, R6          ; temp = dividend >> i
        AND R6, %D 1, R6        ; temp = (dividend >> i) & 1
        OR R2, R6, R2           ; remainder |= (dividend >> i) & 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L10              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        MOVE %D 1, R6
        SHL R6, R3, R6          ; R6 = 1 << i
        OR R0, R6, R0           ; integer_part |= (1 << i)
L10                             ; skip subtraction
        SUB R3, %D 1, R3        ; i--
        JP L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; integer division done
; 16-step fractional refinement loop
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; frac = 0 (fractional accumulator)
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; i = 0 (loop counter, use R6 temporarily)
L11                             ; fractional refinement loop
        CMP R6, %D 16
        JP_SGE L12              ; if i >= 16, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; frac <<= 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L13              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        OR R3, %D 1, R3         ; frac |= 1
L13                             ; skip subtraction
        ADD R6, %D 1, R6        ; i++
        JP L11                  ; continue loop
L12                             ; fractional loop done
; Combine integer_part and frac into Q16.16 result
        SHL R0, %D 16, R0       ; integer_part <<= 16
        OR R0, R3, R0           ; result_abs = (integer_part << 16) | frac
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L14               ; sign == 0 → result 0
        JP_SLT L15              ; sign < 0 → negate
        JP L16                  ; sign > 0 → no change
L14                             ; sign zero

        JP L16
L15                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L16                             ; sign applied

        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 393216, R0      ; load float constant 6.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FDIV             ; call F_FDIV
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FDIV                          ; Helper function: float div(float a, float b)
; F_FDIV: Q16.16 fixed-point division using 32-bit arithmetic
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a / b (Q16.16 format)
; 
; CRITICAL: R5 is reserved as frame pointer and must NEVER be used
; as a general-purpose register. All temporaries use R0-R4, R6-R7.
; All conditional jumps use JP_* mnemonics (not JR_*) for consistency
; with compiler-generated code.
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (dividend, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (divisor, Q16.16)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JP_EQ L5                ; a == 0 → sign = 0
        JP_SLT L4               ; a < 0 → negate
        JP L3                   ; a > 0 → nothing
L5                              ; a is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (result will be 0)
        JP L3
L4                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L3                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
; 32-bit unsigned division: integer_part = a_abs / b_abs
        MOVE R0, R4             ; save dividend a_abs in R4
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; integer_part = 0 (quotient)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; remainder = 0
        MOVE %D 31, R3          ; i = 31 (loop counter)
L8                              ; 32-bit integer division loop
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JP_SLT L9               ; if i < 0, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        MOVE R4, R6             ; temp = dividend (use R6 temporarily)
        SHR R6, R3, R6          ; temp = dividend >> i
        AND R6, %D 1, R6        ; temp = (dividend >> i) & 1
        OR R2, R6, R2           ; remainder |= (dividend >> i) & 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L10              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        MOVE %D 1, R6
        SHL R6, R3, R6          ; R6 = 1 << i
        OR R0, R6, R0           ; integer_part |= (1 << i)
L10                             ; skip subtraction
        SUB R3, %D 1, R3        ; i--
        JP L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; integer division done
; 16-step fractional refinement loop
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; frac = 0 (fractional accumulator)
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; i = 0 (loop counter, use R6 temporarily)
L11                             ; fractional refinement loop
        CMP R6, %D 16
        JP_SGE L12              ; if i >= 16, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; frac <<= 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L13              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        OR R3, %D 1, R3         ; frac |= 1
L13                             ; skip subtraction
        ADD R6, %D 1, R6        ; i++
        JP L11                  ; continue loop
L12                             ; fractional loop done
; Combine integer_part and frac into Q16.16 result
        SHL R0, %D 16, R0       ; integer_part <<= 16
        OR R0, R3, R0           ; result_abs = (integer_part << 16) | frac
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L14               ; sign == 0 → result 0
        JP_SLT L15              ; sign < 0 → negate
        JP L16                  ; sign > 0 → no change
L14                             ; sign zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        JP L16
L15                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L16                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L2                              ; division by zero or dividend is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for division by zero or zero dividend
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return

Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 393216, R0      ; load float constant 6.0 (Q16.16)
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FDIV             ; call F_FDIV
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FDIV                          ; Helper function: float div(float a, float b)
; F_FDIV: Q16.16 fixed-point division using 32-bit arithmetic
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a / b (Q16.16 format)
; 
; CRITICAL: R5 is reserved as frame pointer and must NEVER be used
; as a general-purpose register. All temporaries use R0-R4, R6-R7.
; All conditional jumps use JP_* mnemonics (not JR_*) for consistency
; with compiler-generated code.
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (dividend, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (divisor, Q16.16)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JP_EQ L5                ; a == 0 → sign = 0
        JP_SLT L4               ; a < 0 → negate
        JP L3                   ; a > 0 → nothing
L5                              ; a is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (result will be 0)
        JP L3
L4                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L3                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
; 32-bit unsigned division: integer_part = a_abs / b_abs
        MOVE R0, R4             ; save dividend a_abs in R4
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; integer_part = 0 (quotient)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; remainder = 0
        MOVE %D 31, R3          ; i = 31 (loop counter)
L8                              ; 32-bit integer division loop
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JP_SLT L9               ; if i < 0, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        MOVE R4, R6             ; temp = dividend (use R6 temporarily)
        SHR R6, R3, R6          ; temp = dividend >> i
        AND R6, %D 1, R6        ; temp = (dividend >> i) & 1
        OR R2, R6, R2           ; remainder |= (dividend >> i) & 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L10              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        MOVE %D 1, R6
        SHL R6, R3, R6          ; R6 = 1 << i
        OR R0, R6, R0           ; integer_part |= (1 << i)
L10                             ; skip subtraction
        SUB R3, %D 1, R3        ; i--
        JP L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; integer division done
; 16-step fractional refinement loop
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; frac = 0 (fractional accumulator)
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; i = 0 (loop counter, use R6 temporarily)
L11                             ; fractional refinement loop
        CMP R6, %D 16
        JP_SGE L12              ; if i >= 16, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; frac <<= 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L13              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        OR R3, %D 1, R3         ; frac |= 1
L13                             ; skip subtraction
        ADD R6, %D 1, R6        ; i++
        JP L11                  ; continue loop
L12                             ; fractional loop done
; Combine integer_part and frac into Q16.16 result
        SHL R0, %D 16, R0       ; integer_part <<= 16
        OR R0, R3, R0           ; result_abs = (integer_part << 16) | frac
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L14               ; sign == 0 → result 0
        JP_SLT L15              ; sign < 0 → negate
        JP L16                  ; sign > 0 → no change
L14                             ; sign zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        JP L16
L15                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L16                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L2                              ; division by zero or dividend is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for division by zero or zero dividend
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
FRISC Execution
Expected Actual (Q16.16) Q16.16 Float Status

Generated FRISC Assembly Code 
; Program entry point
        MOVE 40000, R7          ; init stack pointer
        CALL F_MAIN             ; call main
        HALT                    ; end of program, R6 holds return value

; Function definitions
F_MAIN                          ; Function main
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
; Compound statement
        MOVE %D 393216, R0      ; load float constant 6.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        PUSH R0                 ; save left operand
        MOVE %D 131072, R0      ; load float constant 2.0 (Q16.16)
        MOVE %D 0, R1           ; zero for negation
        SUB R1, R0, R0          ; unary minus
        MOVE R0, R1             ; right operand to R1
        POP R0                  ; restore left operand
        PUSH R1                 ; push second arg
        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FDIV             ; call F_FDIV
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FDIV                          ; Helper function: float div(float a, float b)
; F_FDIV: Q16.16 fixed-point division using 32-bit arithmetic
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a / b (Q16.16 format)
; 
; CRITICAL: R5 is reserved as frame pointer and must NEVER be used
; as a general-purpose register. All temporaries use R0-R4, R6-R7.
; All conditional jumps use JP_* mnemonics (not JR_*) for consistency
; with compiler-generated code.
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (dividend, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (divisor, Q16.16)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JP_EQ L5                ; a == 0 → sign = 0
        JP_SLT L4               ; a < 0 → negate
        JP L3                   ; a > 0 → nothing
L5                              ; a is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (result will be 0)
        JP L3
L4                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L3                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
; 32-bit unsigned division: integer_part = a_abs / b_abs
        MOVE R0, R4             ; save dividend a_abs in R4
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; integer_part = 0 (quotient)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; remainder = 0
        MOVE %D 31, R3          ; i = 31 (loop counter)
L8                              ; 32-bit integer division loop
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JP_SLT L9               ; if i < 0, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        MOVE R4, R6             ; temp = dividend (use R6 temporarily)
        SHR R6, R3, R6          ; temp = dividend >> i
        AND R6, %D 1, R6        ; temp = (dividend >> i) & 1
        OR R2, R6, R2           ; remainder |= (dividend >> i) & 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L10              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        MOVE %D 1, R6
        SHL R6, R3, R6          ; R6 = 1 << i
        OR R0, R6, R0           ; integer_part |= (1 << i)
L10                             ; skip subtraction
        SUB R3, %D 1, R3        ; i--
        JP L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; integer division done
; 16-step fractional refinement loop
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; frac = 0 (fractional accumulator)
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; i = 0 (loop counter, use R6 temporarily)
L11                             ; fractional refinement loop
        CMP R6, %D 16
        JP_SGE L12              ; if i >= 16, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; frac <<= 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L13              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        OR R3, %D 1, R3         ; frac |= 1
L13                             ; skip subtraction
        ADD R6, %D 1, R6        ; i++
        JP L11                  ; continue loop
L12                             ; fractional loop done
; Combine integer_part and frac into Q16.16 result
        SHL R0, %D 16, R0       ; integer_part <<= 16
        OR R0, R3, R0           ; result_abs = (integer_part << 16) | frac
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L14               ; sign == 0 → result 0
        JP_SLT L15              ; sign < 0 → negate
        JP L16                  ; sign > 0 → no change
L14                             ; sign zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        JP L16
L15                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L16                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L2                              ; division by zero or dividend is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for division by zero or zero dividend
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
FRISC Execution
Expected Actual (Q16.16) Q16.16 Float Status
— 196608 3.0 ❔ UNKNOWN

        PUSH R0                 ; push first arg
        CALL F_FDIV             ; call F_FDIV
        ADD R7, %D 8, R7        ; cleanup arguments
        MOVE R6, R0             ; move result to R0
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value
        JP L1                   ; jump to function exit
L1                              ; function exit
        POP R5                  ; restore old frame pointer
        RET                     ; return to caller

; Global variables

; Float helper functions (IEEE-754 single-precision)

F_FDIV                          ; Helper function: float div(float a, float b)
; F_FDIV: Q16.16 fixed-point division using 32-bit arithmetic
; Input:  a at (R5+08), b at (R5+0C)
; Output: R6 = a / b (Q16.16 format)
; 
; CRITICAL: R5 is reserved as frame pointer and must NEVER be used
; as a general-purpose register. All temporaries use R0-R4, R6-R7.
; All conditional jumps use JP_* mnemonics (not JR_*) for consistency
; with compiler-generated code.
        PUSH R5                 ; save old frame pointer
        MOVE R7, R5             ; R5 = current SP -> base of frame
        LOAD R0, (R5+08)        ; a (dividend, Q16.16)
        LOAD R1, (R5+0C)        ; b (divisor, Q16.16)
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if b == 0, return 0
        MOVE %D 1, R4           ; sign = +1
        CMP R0, %D 0
        JP_EQ L5                ; a == 0 → sign = 0
        JP_SLT L4               ; a < 0 → negate
        JP L3                   ; a > 0 → nothing
L5                              ; a is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R4           ; sign = 0 (result will be 0)
        JP L3
L4                              ; negate a
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0 (a_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L3                              ; a sign handled
        CMP R1, %D 0
        JP_SGE L6               ; b >= 0 → nothing
L7                              ; negate b
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R1, R1          ; R1 = -R1 (b_abs)
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R4, R4          ; sign = -sign
L6                              ; b sign handled
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L2                ; if sign == 0, result is 0
        PUSH R4                 ; save sign on stack
; 32-bit unsigned division: integer_part = a_abs / b_abs
        MOVE R0, R4             ; save dividend a_abs in R4
        MOVE %D 0, R0           ; integer_part = 0 (quotient)
        MOVE %D 0, R2           ; remainder = 0
        MOVE %D 31, R3          ; i = 31 (loop counter)
L8                              ; 32-bit integer division loop
        CMP R3, %D 0
        JP_SLT L9               ; if i < 0, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        MOVE R4, R6             ; temp = dividend (use R6 temporarily)
        SHR R6, R3, R6          ; temp = dividend >> i
        AND R6, %D 1, R6        ; temp = (dividend >> i) & 1
        OR R2, R6, R2           ; remainder |= (dividend >> i) & 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L10              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        MOVE %D 1, R6
        SHL R6, R3, R6          ; R6 = 1 << i
        OR R0, R6, R0           ; integer_part |= (1 << i)
L10                             ; skip subtraction
        SUB R3, %D 1, R3        ; i--
        JP L8                   ; continue loop
L9                              ; integer division done
; 16-step fractional refinement loop
        MOVE %D 0, R3           ; frac = 0 (fractional accumulator)
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; i = 0 (loop counter, use R6 temporarily)
L11                             ; fractional refinement loop
        CMP R6, %D 16
        JP_SGE L12              ; if i >= 16, done
        SHL R2, %D 1, R2        ; remainder <<= 1
        SHL R3, %D 1, R3        ; frac <<= 1
        CMP R2, R1
        JP_SLT L13              ; if remainder < b_abs, skip subtraction
        SUB R2, R1, R2          ; remainder -= b_abs
        OR R3, %D 1, R3         ; frac |= 1
L13                             ; skip subtraction
        ADD R6, %D 1, R6        ; i++
        JP L11                  ; continue loop
L12                             ; fractional loop done
; Combine integer_part and frac into Q16.16 result
        SHL R0, %D 16, R0       ; integer_part <<= 16
        OR R0, R3, R0           ; result_abs = (integer_part << 16) | frac
; Apply sign to result
        POP R4                  ; restore sign from stack
        CMP R4, %D 0
        JP_EQ L14               ; sign == 0 → result 0
        JP_SLT L15              ; sign < 0 → negate
        JP L16                  ; sign > 0 → no change
L14                             ; sign zero
        MOVE %D 0, R0
        JP L16
L15                             ; negate result
        MOVE %D 0, R2
        SUB R2, R0, R0          ; R0 = -R0
L16                             ; sign applied
        MOVE R0, R6             ; return value in R6
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
L2                              ; division by zero or dividend is zero
        MOVE %D 0, R6           ; return 0 for division by zero or zero dividend
        POP R5                  ; restore frame pointer
        RET                     ; return
FRISC Execution
Expected Actual (Q16.16) Q16.16 Float Status
— 163840 2.5 ❔ UNKNOWN

Note: Float values are compared using Q16.16 fixed-point representation. Q16.16 Float column shows the converted float value for comparison.
